PI_CONTACT_INFO: Address: 902 Battelle Blvd.,P. O. Box 999, MSIN K9-30, Richland, WA 99354; e-mail: mikhail.pekour@pnnl.gov
PLATFORM: DOE Research Aircraft G-1, N701BN
LOCATION: Flights based out of Bartlesville, OK. Local time=UTC-5 hrs.
ASSOCIATED_DATA: Aicraft location and attitude data are in IWG1 file: 20160425a.iwg1.a1.ver2.txt; flight notes are on the wiki site; readme file is with the data set.
INSTRUMENT_INFO: The CVI Inlet (BMI, www.brechtel.com) was used to collect larger cloud elements (droplets and/or ice crystals) for real time analysis of dried resedues.
DATA_INFO: The CVI inlet was used in two modes: regular CVI operation and "under-kinetic" mode, when no or insufficient Add Flow was supplied; in this case the inlet did not work as size separator but rather like conventional forward facing ("under-kinetic") inlet with dilution due to Flush and Add (if any) flows. Variable "CVI_mode" indicates inlet  status (0 - CVI mode, 1 - under-kinetic mode, -1 - transition between the modes
